Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on fields structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 335. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 7 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on fields structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 335. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 7. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 220.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Given a fields algebra A over field F with spectral property conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 335. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 732/3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 335 is immediate from the spectral property property. For the inductive step, we decompose the fields structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 732. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
